<h1 align="center">OSTDB</h1>
<p align="center">Community-curated video game soundtrack links.</p>
<p align="center"><a href="https://ostdb.net">ostdb.net</a></p>

---

This list is auto-generated from the [OSTDB](https://ostdb.net) database, one commit per soundtrack. Found a game missing a soundtrack link, or one you want to correct? [Submit it on ostdb.net](https://ostdb.net).

## Submit via GitHub

You can also submit or correct a soundtrack by opening a pull request — no need to use the website. See **[SUBMITTING.md](SUBMITTING.md)** for instructions.
